# feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak — as built

Baseline: none (changes `launch-2026-11-20-ujai` in place, P29) · KICKOFF.md approved 2026-11-24 · kickoff started 2026-11-24

Run mode ([P102](plans/decisions.md)): the whole ROUTE R1–R63 without a per-row stop, at the owner's word. Each row ends
with its row below and a one-line report; the `Reviewed` cells stay `—` until the owner reviews at Close. Where agent-os
cannot build `plans/routes-final.md` exactly as written, the row follows agent-os, records the difference here and in
`plans/decisions.md` (AMBER, "user delegated 2026-11-24") and syncs `routes-final.md`; a scope change is parked as
`deferred` for the owner, never decided. Commits stay local (no push).

## Kickoff choices

- P22 — public-id prefixes of the new tables: recorded per row as each table lands. R1: none new (`billing.plans` keeps `pln_`).
- R1 — `PLAN_FEATURE_KEYS` (P103): `email_ask`, `phone_ask`, `reply_like_person`, `multiple_public_reply_lines`, `message_versions`, `broadcasts`, `segments`, `past_comments`, `extra_delay` (PRO), `starter_questions`, `mark_seen`.
- worker-changes note 1 — the launch's "start the automations queued behind an email" kept unchanged.
- R2 — no table, so no public-id prefix (`channelConnection`'s prefix is R3's). Circuit `meta`, outbound integration `meta-graph`, Redis keys `app:channel-connect-state:<state>` and `app:meta_usage:<organization>:<connection>` (P107–P109).
- R34 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. New SECURITY DEFINER readers `app.list_organization_counts_for_user`, `tenancy.resolve_main_roles_for_user`; the counts port `OrganizationCountsPort` (P407).
- R12 — no new table, so no public-id prefix; the message-file read for later rows is `UploadService.findUsableMessageFile` (R13, R20).
- R20 — no new public-id prefix (`app.instagram_attachments` has none). Seams for later rows: `InstagramSendAccessService` (`readSendAccess`, `readSendPace`, `buildUsageRecorder` — R42–R45 before every send), `InstagramAttachmentService.findOrUploadAttachment` / `forgetAttachment` (R42's send of a file and its `attachment_failed`), `uploadAttachments` + `InstagramAttachmentUploadQueue` (R46's `attachment-upload` job, WC-32).
- R3 — `channelConnection` → `chc` (P22; the placeholder R2's tests used); `app.channel_connection_tokens` and `app.channel_connection_usages` have no public id. Advisory-lock namespace `CHANNEL_ACCOUNT_LIMIT` (`CHAP`). Tenancy migration `20261124101530_tenancy_permission_channel_account.sql` (lane A; lane B's tenancy migrations must sort around it).
- R5 — `channelAccount` → `cha` (P22). The channel account service is the connection's account port; the later rows' seams: `ChannelAccountLanePort` (R18), `ChannelAccountPlanStopPort` (R18, R24), `ChannelAccountService.canChannelAccountUseFeature` / `listChannelAccountFeatureProblems` (2, 4, 6, 7 — map a refusal with `channelFeatureRefusalError`), `markAccountBroken` (1, 5, 6), test factories `insertChannelAccount` / `organizationChannelAccountPath`.
- R4 — no new table, so no public-id prefix; reason `ad_account_not_found`, `meta.ad_access` (`granted` · `missing`).
- R24 — `trialAccount` → `tra` (P22). Billing's seams for later rows: `SubscriptionService.emitPlanStateChanged({ organizationPublicId, change, previous, keptChannelAccountPublicIds })` (R26's four Stripe cases, R52's grace end and legacy-plan finding), the trial port `SubscriptionTrialService` (wired), the accounts port `BillingChannelAccountsPort` (wired, `listActiveChannelAccounts` gives `instagramUserId` and `connectedAt` for WC-38's oldest-kept rule), `ChannelAccountPlanStopService` (R18 adds `plan_limit`). Event type `subscription.plan_state_changed` (`PLAN_STATE_CHANGE`).
- R26 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. The webhook's plan-state cases emit through `SubscriptionService.emitPlanStateChangedAfterCommit` (WC-38's trigger, R52's backup check reuses `emitPlanStateChanged`).
- R21 — `segment` → `seg` (P22). Later rows' seams: `SegmentBackgroundCountPort` (R46 — the `segment-count` task and its Redis copy), `SegmentDeleteStep` (R22 — empty drafts' `segment_id` in the delete's transaction); `buildSegmentAudienceCondition` is the audience expression R22 / R46's broadcast audience reads; R13 updates `BroadcastSegmentRepository.findAccountAutomationIds` when automations are keyed by the channel account; R14 / R19 complete `got_message` numbers (P310).
- R27 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. Lane B's decision range starts at P201 (R12's decision renumbered P103 → P201).
- R28 — no new table; the Viewer rows the migration inserts take the existing `memberRole` prefix `rol_`. For R3: add the `channel-account:*` codes to `APP_PERMISSIONS` and name their holders in `APP_PERMISSION_DEFAULT_TEAM_GRANTS` (`channel-account:read` → Editor, Viewer; `:manage` → none) — typecheck refuses the code until then; the reconcile grants them to existing teams on deploy, beside R3's own migration.
- R29 — no new table, so no public-id prefix (route 118 addresses the invitation by its existing `inv_` id). For R34/R36 and R30: the shared optional sign-in for a PUBLIC route is `authenticateWhenBearerPresent` (`src/shared/middlewares/core/auth.middleware.ts`) — 10.A's OAuth link start reuses it.
- R30 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. For R32: the strong step-up without 2FA (AT15) — `STEP_UP_FACTORS` has no `password`, so `EmailLoginService.verifyCodeForStepUp` + the `email_code` factor is the only non-MFA step-up; route 127 makes it strong and gives it reasons; the k6 scenarios `comprehensive-journey`, `auth-security-lifecycle`, `fe-full-surface`, `fe-login-to-organization` (`AUTH=password`) move to it. For R31: route 125's `email_code_always_on` is built (`AuthMethodService.delete`); GitHub stays.
- R31 — no new table, so no public-id prefix (a linked Apple way takes the existing `authMethod` prefix). Apple's name waits at `oauth:provider-user:<state>`; the one method→status exception is `METHOD_STATUS_POLICY_EXCEPTIONS`.
- R32 — `auth.trusted_devices` takes the prefix `tdv_` (`PUBLIC_ID_PREFIXES.trustedDevice`, P22) and the advisory-lock namespace `TRUSTED_DEVICE` (`TDEV`). For R50: the trusted-device clean-up calls `TrustedDeviceRepository.deleteEndedBefore({ databaseHandle, cutoff })` inside `MAINTENANCE_SCOPE.SESSION_RETENTION_CLEANUP` with cutoff now − `TRUSTED_DEVICE_ENDED_KEEP_DAYS`. For R33: `DELETE /auth/me/sessions` (route 144) already ends every trusted device but this browser's.
- R33 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. The session place file is `SESSION_PLACE_DATABASE_PATH` (unset → empty place); the API image carries it when built with `SESSION_PLACE_DATABASE_URL`.

- R36 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. The check-before-send interface is `createAndDispatchNotification({ input: DispatchNotificationInput })` (`src/domains/notify/sub-domains/notification/notification-dispatch.service.ts`): `recipientUserIds`, `organization_id`, `type`, `title`, `message`, `action_url?`, `channels` (`in_app` · `email`), `locale`, `dedupe_key` (required); for background work `NotificationService.createOrganizationNotifications({ scope, recipientUserIds, notification, dedupeKey })`. The kinds are `NOTIFICATION_KINDS`.
- R25 — no new table, so no public-id prefix; `{payment_method_id}` is Stripe's `pm_…` id, the third non-entity path parameter (P78, `PAYMENT_METHOD_ID_PATTERN`). For R26/R52: the last-card rule reads `CARD_REQUIRED_SUBSCRIPTION_STATUSES` (`subscription.constants.ts`).
- R35 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. The confirm-email interface for later rows is `EmailConfirmationService` on `AuthContainer` (`requestLink`, `confirm`); its email is always sent (never in the notification grid, 11.10).
- R16 — no new public-id prefix (`app.instagram_webhook_event_keys` has none; events keep `iwe_`). The part maker for later split tables is `app.fn_create_daily_partitions(parent regclass, from_day date, to_day date)` (UTC days, `app` range-split tables on a `date` or `timestamptz` key; a plain invoker function, EXECUTE revoked from PUBLIC — owner members only). The store function for R17 / R39 (missed comments) / automation's past-comments read is `InstagramWebhookEventService.recordReceivedEvents({ scope, channelAccount: { organizationId, channelAccountId }, requestId, events, now? })`.
- R17 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. For R19: implement `InstagramOwnSentMessageLookup` (`instagram-webhook.types.ts`) as `InstagramMessageService.findOwnSentMessageIds` and pass it as the `InstagramWebhookService`'s fourth constructor argument in `app.container.ts`. Unsigned-delivery limiter key prefix `instagram-webhook-unsigned:`; route flag `overloadShedLast`.
- R6 — no new table, so no public-id prefix; `PUBLIC_ID_PREFIXES.instagramAccount` (`iga`) removed — the account's one id is `cha_` (P47). The extension's writer is `InstagramAccountExtensionService` (`createAtPick`, `recordWebhookSubscribed`, `clearOnRevoke`, `eraseProfile`, `findByChannelAccountIds`); its object `serializeInstagramAccountExtension`; the team delete's connection step `ChannelConnectionTokenService.revokeOrganizationConnections`; test helper `buildInstagramPickValues`.
- R10 — `app.contact_activities` and `app.contact_tag_counts` have no public id (never returned alone); contacts keep `con_`. Seams for later rows: `ContactTagEventRecorder` (`recordContactTagEvents`, R11 — pass as `ports.tagEventRecorder` of `ContactService`), `ContactBulkActionQueue` (`enqueueContactBulkAction`, R46 — `ports.bulkActionQueue`; the piece size `CONTACT_BULK_ACTION_PIECE_SIZE`), `ContactPauseStep` / `ContactErasureStep` lists in `app.container.ts` (6's waiting-message skip and cancel, 3.B's tag-event delete), the 3.A.10 operations for intake (R16/R17), the run engine (R14: `recordContactFirstRun`, `findContactSendState`, `addContactTags({ sourceAutomationId, chainNumber })`), sending (`recordContactOpeningMessage`) and broadcasts (`recordContactBroadcastSent`); `first_automation_id`'s FK for R13.
- R9 — no new table, so no public-id prefix; new index `idx_channel_accounts_provider_account`, new resolvers `app.resolve_organization_public_ids_for_provider_account` / `…_for_provider_business` (P211).
- R8 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. The pickers' break port is `InstagramMediaConnectionBreakPort` (`instagram-media.types.ts`, R4's `markConnectionBroken` shape); the watch's writers are `InstagramMediaService.updateCommentWatch` / `recordLiveEnded`; the account read for an Instagram route by `cha_` id is `InstagramAccountService.findAccountReadAccess` with `ChannelConnectionTokenService.findConnectionAccess`.
- R11 — `app.contact_tag_events` has no public id (never returned; its `id` is part of a tag start's once key). Seams for later rows: `ContactTagEventIntakeQueue` (`enqueueContactTagEventIntake({ organizationPublicId, contactPublicIds })`, R38 — pass as `new ContactTagEventService(repository, { intakeQueue })` in `createContactServices`; with it, `queued_at` is stamped in the tag commit); `createWorkerContactTagEventRepository` and the indexes `idx_contact_tag_events_pickup` / `_created` for the pick-up (WC-27, R47) and the 8-day clean-up (WC-54, R50; the policy's maintenance arm is `app.global_retention_cleanup`); `contactTagEventService` on `appDomain`; `source_automation_id`'s key for R13's automations migration (`ON DELETE SET NULL (source_automation_id)`, composite → `app.automations (organization_id, id)`).
- R7 — no new table, so no public-id prefix (route 12 takes the account's `cha_` id). Route 12's seams for later rows: `InstagramAccountSettingsLanePort` (`wakeLanes`, R18 — pass it as the settings service's fourth constructor argument), the settings-file values in `instagram-account-settings.constants.ts` (R18 moves them into sub-domain 6's settings file, P4), `InstagramAccountSettingsService.findSettings({ scope, channelAccount })` / `createWorkerInstagramAccountSettingsRepository(...).findByChannelAccount(...)` for event processing and the lanes; test helpers `instagramAccountSettingsPath`, `organizationInstagramAccountSettingsPath`, `buildInstagramAccountSettingsRow`.
- R18 — six new tables, none with a public id (route 63 takes the account's `cha_` id; `igm` is R19's message). New maintenance kind `SENDING_STATE` (GUC `app.sending_state_access`, worker context `sending_state`). Redis key `app:message_month_count:<organization public id>` (10 s). Env `SENDING_ENABLED`, `SENDING_ENVIRONMENT_ID`. Command `pnpm ops:sending-state [--minimum <n>]`. OpenAPI tag `Instagram sending`. The sending settings file is `app/sub-domains/instagram-sending/instagram-sending.constants.ts`.
- R13 — no new public-id prefix (automations keep `aut_`; counts and reply states have none). Seams for later rows: `AutomationRunAutomationRepository` (the engine's step view — R14 replaces it with `steps`), `AutomationCountService.increment` (R14 — runs started, taps, typed answers per version, in the event's commit), `AutomationReplyStateService` (6's reply lane), `app.channel-ports.ts` `ChannelSendingPort` (R19–R20 implement and wire `channelSendingPorts` / `AutomationServicePorts.sendingPorts`), `AutomationStarterQuestionSyncQueue` (R40), `open_backfill` (R15), routes 36–37 (R19); `matchAutomationKeyword` (intake's and route 35's one matcher).
- R15 — `automationBackfill` → `abf` (P22), path id `{automation_backfill_id}`. Seams for later rows: `AutomationBackfillReadQueue.enqueueAutomationBackfillRead({ organizationPublicId, channelAccountPublicId, automationBackfillPublicId })` (R41 wires it into `AutomationBackfillService`'s 5th argument in `app.container.ts`), `AutomationBackfillRepository` (R41 adds the claim, counters and end), `AUTOMATION_BACKFILL_OPEN_STATUSES` and the watch / queued indexes; test factories `insertAutomationBackfill`, `automationBackfillsPath`.
- R19 — public-id prefix **`igm`** (`PUBLIC_ID_PREFIXES.instagramMessage`, OpenAPI path label "Instagram message"); attempts have none. Week part maker `app.fn_create_weekly_partitions(parent regclass, from_day date, to_day date)` (ISO weeks, `<table>_pIYYYwIW`). Audit action `app.instagram_message.retry`. Seams: `InstagramMessageService.claimMessage` / `saveSendResult` / `moveMessageLane` (R42's send core), `InstagramMessageRetryQueue` (R46), `retryAllowedFailedMessages` (R46's job body).
- R14 — no new public-id prefix (`app.automation_run_open_keys` has none; runs keep `run_`, comment logs `acl_`). The month part maker for later split tables is `app.fn_create_monthly_partitions(parent regclass, from_month date, to_month date)` (UTC months, `<table>_pYYYYMM`). Seams for later rows: `ChannelSendingPort` implemented by 6 (R19–R20 — set `channelSendingPorts.instagram`; a follow check's `sent` result carries `reason` `following` / `not_following`; `ChannelMessageResultInput.scope`), `AutomationRunService.recordMessageResult` (6's result save), the entry points with normalized kinds for intake per kind (R38 — `startCommentRun` `kind` / `parentCommentId` / `isOwnComment` / `adId` / `mediaPublishedAt`, `handleDirectMessage` `kind` / `storyId` / `sharedMediaId` / `isFirstMessage` / `isOwnerChatting`, `handleButtonClick` `kind` / `starterQuestion` / `refCode` / `adId`, `startTagRun`; past comments of R15 call `startCommentRun`), `AutomationRunService.repairOpenRunKeys({ databaseHandle, limit })` (WC-67 at R47), the three split tables for the upkeep (R49 — drop runs + run-events parts 98 days after their month, comment-log parts 90).
- R37 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. New queue family `instagram-send` (the send service `worker-send`), limiter name `instagram-database`, job names `instagram-send-lane-turn` / `instagram-send-fast-message` (task job names are their owners'). Seams for later rows: `appDomain.instagramSendWork` — `laneTurn` (`InstagramLaneTurnWork.workLaneTurn`, R42–R45: claim with `claimLaneTurn`, release with `releaseLaneTurn`), `fastMessage` (`InstagramFastMessageWork.sendFastMessage`, R42 / R44), `taskHandlers[jobName]` (`InstagramTaskHandler`, R40 / R41 / R46); producers `enqueueInstagramSendLaneTurn`, `enqueueInstagramSendFastMessage`, `enqueueInstagramTask`; every unit of work through `context.runInDatabaseSlot`; intake (R38) takes `getInstagramDatabaseSlotLimiter()` and sets the webhook registration's `databaseSlotLimiterName`.
- R40 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. Job names `instagram-account-setup`, `instagram-webhook-subscription-check`, `instagram-starter-question-sync` (task queue), sweep job `sweep-instagram-webhook-subscriptions` (scheduler id `instagram-webhook-subscription-sweep`); job ids `setup-<cha_…>-<ms>-try-<n>`, `starters-<cha_…>-<ms>-try-<n>`, `subscription-check-<cha_…>-<window ms>`; the ice-breaker payload `starter:<aut_…>`.
- R49 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. Function `app.fn_manage_time_partitions(regclass, integer, integer, boolean)` (the one maintenance-only SECURITY DEFINER), scope `MAINTENANCE_SCOPE.APP_PARTITION_UPKEEP`, queue `app-partition-upkeep`, job names `make-app-partitions` / `drop-expired-app-partitions`, scheduler ids `daily-app-partition-make` / `hourly-app-partition-drop`, gauges `app_partition_ready_days` / `app_partition_default_rows` / `app_partition_oldest_overdue_seconds`, alert messages `app.partition_upkeep.*`.

The broadcast button payload `<brc_…>::<igm_…>` (`buildBroadcastButtonPayload` / `parseBroadcastButtonPayload` in `broadcast/broadcast-recipient/broadcast-recipient-button-payload.util.ts`). - R43 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. Seams for later rows: `InstagramSendLaneRules` per lane in `createInstagramSendWork`'s `laneRules` (R43 slow / reply, R44 fast, R45 broadcast); `InstagramSendCoreService.sendMessage` (one message through the gates, the call and the save — the repair's and the lanes' entry); `InstagramSendGateService.claimSend`; `decideInstagramSendOutcome` / `stopDurationOf` (the repair's settle and the outage watch, R47–R48); `SendingStateService.recordAppLimit` / `readAllAccountsStop`.

Seams for later rows: `BroadcastSendQueuePort` (`enqueueBroadcastAudience`, `enqueueBroadcastMatchedCount` with `{ organizationPublicId, broadcastPublicId, channelAccountPublicId }` — R46 passes it as `BroadcastServicePorts.sendQueue` in `createBroadcastServices`); the start and end duties (WC-28, WC-29) and `handleChatOpened` (R47) read `idx_broadcasts_scheduled_at`, `idx_broadcasts_waiting_ends_at`, `idx_broadcasts_to_settle`, `idx_broadcasts_account_waiting_for_chats`; the purge (R50) `idx_broadcasts_tombstoned`; recipients (R23) take `idx_broadcasts_organization_id_id` as their parent key.

- R53 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. Jobs `sweep-weekly-summaries` / `send-weekly-summary` / `notification-contact-details` on `notification`; `check-owner-problem-emails` / `check-month-limit-notices` on `instagram-webhook`; notice keys `month-limit:<month>:<line>`, `owner-problem:<cha_…>:<kind>:<due ms>`, `weekly:<org>:<Monday>`, `contact-details:<cha_…>:<window ms>`, `backfill-done:<abf_…>`, team `team-alert:<key>:<address>`.
- R41 — no new table, so no public-id prefix. Task job names `automation-backfill-read` (ids `automation-backfill-read-<cha_…>-<uuid>`, priority 20) and `next-post-linking` (ids `next-post-linking-<cha_…>-<15-min round>`, priority 10) on `instagram-tasks`; repeatables `watch-automation-backfill-reads` (`*/10`) and `sweep-next-post-linking` (`*/15`) on `instagram-webhook`; advisory lock namespace `AUTOMATION_BACKFILL_READ` (`ABFR`). Seams for later rows: `AutomationBackfillService.failAutomationBackfillsForPlanFeatureOff({ scope, organizationId, channelAccountIds? })` (R52's move down, WC-38); the read's end log `app.automation_backfill.read_finished` (R53's `automation.backfill_done` notice, AC-8.8).

- R47 — no new public-id prefix and no new queue. Job names (SN6) and schedulers `instagram-<job name>`, every minute: on `instagram-tasks` `repair-lost-turns`, `repair-stuck-sends`, `repair-expiry`, `repair-closed-windows`, `repair-reply-trim`, `repair-paused-cancel`, `repair-counts`, `repair-broadcast-start`, `repair-broadcast-end`; on `instagram-webhook` `repair-tag-event-pickup`, `repair-open-run-keys`. Metrics `repair_duty_round_seconds{duty}`, `repair_duty_backlog{duty}`. Seams for later rows: `runRepairDutyRound` / `runRepairDutyPieces` (R48's watches, R50's clean-ups), `InstagramMessageService.settleWaitingMessages`, `addMissingInstagramSendLaneTurns` / `addMissingInstagramSendFastMessages`, `BroadcastRecipientService.handleChatOpened`.

- R50 — no prefix. For R61 (used at R61) / R62: the hourly clean-ups are `runAppTableCleanUpJob`, `runAutomationTombstoneRetentionJob`, `runSessionCleanupJob` (`now` injectable); the gauge is `retention_cleanup_oldest_overdue_seconds{table}`; `app.instagram_send_day_counts` is not cleaned (no keep time in the plan, P414 (7)).

R43: the per-automation "chat closed" lines (route 63's `listWindowClosedByAutomation`), the comment re-read before `deleted_comment`, the test's 3-turn limit. ## Summary

Built R1–R62 (62 rows, each with its row above); R54 and R55 (the flows of AC-19.\* and AC-20.\*) landed from lane C
([P351](plans/decisions.md), [P352](plans/decisions.md)) and R63 is this Close. Every row ran under the owner's delegation ([P102](plans/decisions.md)),
so the `Reviewed` cells wait for the owner's review at Close. Merges between the build lanes are logged in
[plans/merge-log.md](plans/merge-log.md). Acceptance: [plans/acceptance.md](plans/acceptance.md) — every item
has a Result (`pass`, `partial` with what is missing, or `open`); the frontend's contract:
[plans/frontend-route-map.md](plans/frontend-route-map.md).

- **Planned, not built (deferred — user OK):** no row is deferred. Inside built rows, not built and waiting for the
  owner: the "Your DMs are safe" banner (AC-27.6) and restore step 4 (AC-27.10) ([P452](plans/decisions.md)); the
  message-access banner's field ([P409](plans/decisions.md)); a picture with text counted as two DMs (AC-17.17,
  [P412](plans/decisions.md)); a team-paused organization tied to its lanes and the context (AC-12.10); "Seen" after a
  reply and the other Meta-gated features until their §43 tests ([P36](plans/decisions.md)); the malware scan
  ([P34](plans/decisions.md)).
- **Built, not planned:** the four password routes kept, disabled behind `PASSWORD_ROUTES_ENABLED` (owner, RED,
  [P251](plans/decisions.md)); every worker in the existing `worker` service (owner, RED, [P140](plans/decisions.md));
  the build lanes' isolated test stacks ([P139](plans/decisions.md)) and the partition-safe test wipe; fixes at source
  found by the flows — the organization delete stops its lanes (R6 × R61), the late fast messages spread after a
  reconnect and the connection-usage read's organization binding ([P252](plans/decisions.md)), the move-down notice
  ([P501](plans/decisions.md)), route 102 replacing the local Free row ([P502](plans/decisions.md)), route 103 refusing
  a paid plan without a Stripe subscription ([P503](plans/decisions.md)), the broadcast's run-ended hook and its done
  note numbers ([P451](plans/decisions.md)), §33.6 rules 2–3 at R62 ([P452](plans/decisions.md)); R54's asks again (3 after the first question), the stop-word cleaning and the automation's `bad_text` stop with its owner notice ([P351](plans/decisions.md)); the OpenAPI
  `409 provider_account_in_use` on the OAuth callback; the RLS fix migration `20261125210000` (R47 × merge round 2,
  [P144](plans/decisions.md)).
- **Follow-ups for the next iteration:** drop the `lav` → `lavender` expand trigger in the release after this one
  ([P202](plans/decisions.md)); open each Meta-gated feature as its §43 test passes ([P36](plans/decisions.md)); the
  malware scan ([P34](plans/decisions.md)); the owner items below that become rows; knip's unused
  `instagram-send-minute-count.repository.ts`; the flaky `request-lifecycle.middleware.unit.test.ts` (`vi.waitFor`
  under load); the `app.container.ts` split (needs the owner's OK).

- **The Close (2026-11-25):** the owner's corner-case review (24 cases: 14 already proved, 7 proved by new tests,
  2 waiting on owner items — a reply to a reply, a role change during a broadcast), which found and fixed an erased
  person still getting a public reply (R43); the owner's renames and UTC pin ([P147](plans/decisions.md)–[P149](plans/decisions.md));
  a check against Meta's Unified Login docs (Oct–Nov 2026) — paging, which answers break what, the login's `config_id`,
  the token's end ([P353](plans/decisions.md)–[P357](plans/decisions.md)); a check against Meta's webhook fields and
  examples — the `comment_id` key, an ad referral keeping her text, the key day across midnight, unsupported and
  handoff messages, reels, unreacts, `{name}` within Meta's limits, the store's process-wide cap
  ([P253](plans/decisions.md)–[P262](plans/decisions.md)). Gates on the final tree: `pnpm ci:local` all steps passed
  (fast 881 files / 7,961 tests; database projects 457 files / 3,942 tests), `pnpm test:rls-role` and `pnpm sonar:scan`
  (results in [plans/merge-log.md](plans/merge-log.md) › Close).

| Sending engine | Messages and attempts, attachments, the send core, the sending state and switch, lane steps, routes 63–64, queues and the database limiter, the slow, reply, fast and broadcast lanes, the watches, the task split, the repair duties | P136–P138, P305–P308, P408, P411–P413, P217, P222, P319 |
| Webhook intake | The day-split events and keys, the receiver, intake per kind, stuck events and missed comments, the past-comments read and "next post" linking, the kept post read, the read's counts; the Close's webhook gap fixes (Meta's 2026 docs) and the store's per-process cap | P303, P304, P311–P316, [P253](plans/decisions.md)–[P262](plans/decisions.md) |

8. P315 — intake's third Meta read (a commented post not stored yet) kept beside the "next post" sweep ([P315](plans/decisions.md)).

10. Reconnect-notice recipients (Owner and Admin, [P216](plans/decisions.md)); whether UN9's Editor reach was meant ([P301](plans/decisions.md)); R36's role-change notice triggers; trial-notice recipients and a move up reactivating accounts ([P220](plans/decisions.md)).
11. WH3 / P35 — an account in `error` still taken in, its events starting runs that wait in stopped lanes ([P312](plans/decisions.md)).
12. Wording conflicts: AC-9.1 vs P76 / BC3 (segments and broadcasts on Free), AC-10.2's month state on route 64, AC-1.1's reason name, AC-11.7.
13. R52's backup gap (a lost `plan_ended`), the 7-day grace lookback ([P219](plans/decisions.md)–[P221](plans/decisions.md)).

16. A reply to a reply answered under the top-level comment needs the parent comment id on the run — a schema change (R43 note, [P411](plans/decisions.md)).
17. An idle lane taking the account test after a stop pushes the stop back 60 s (`idleTurnSeconds`) — confirm it is intended.
18. R54 / R55 acceptance as proven ([P351](plans/decisions.md)): AC-19.10 per CT9 (the tag step lists its own tags), AC-19.13 through the go-live warning, and the ask-again wording per §5.8, not §14 rule 7 — confirm these readings.
19. The AMBER rows of the build (table above) — confirm or change; in particular [P144](plans/decisions.md), [P145](plans/decisions.md), [P501](plans/decisions.md)–[P503](plans/decisions.md).
20. Unified Login token refresh ([P355](plans/decisions.md)): Meta's Business Integration System User Access Token Management API needs `business_management` in the login configuration — add it (and a refresh job) or keep the reconnect a dated token's `token_expires_soon` flag asks for; and which expiry the production configuration chooses.
21. Unified Login cancel and reconnect guidance ([P357](plans/decisions.md)): Meta's `error=access_denied` on a cancelled login lands on meepzo-fe (no backend route) — what it shows; and whether to word the reconnect notice by Meta's 190 subcode once Meta names one for "app removed from the portfolio" (the moved account already has its own `account_not_shared` path).

22. Webhook (owner questions, [P261](plans/decisions.md) and the 2026-11-25 docs review): (a) **no linked Facebook Page** — Meta's temporary limit: an account shared without its Page may get no `messages` webhooks; the workaround (a second Unified Login configuration asking for the Page and `pages_show_list`, Advanced Access, clients reconnect) and a DM backfill through `/{ig-user-id}/conversations` for the gap — not built; (b) **mTLS** on the webhook (Meta's CA `meta-outbound-api-ca-2025-12.pem`, CN `client.webhooks.fbclientcerts.com`) at the load balancer — not configured; (c) **`META_APP_SECRET` rotation** — one secret verifies the signature, so a rotation fails every delivery until the deploy (no second accepted secret); (d) echoes sent by **another app** (`message.app_id` not ours) count as the owner typing (6-hour quiet) — kept; (e) **`standby` / handover**: when another app owns the thread Meta sends `standby[]`, which the receiver ignores; (f) an **unsupported-media** message is skipped, so it neither opens her window nor counts as her first message; (g) a shared **story** (`story` / `ig_story` attachment) stays a plain `message`; (h) an **ad-post comment** names `media.original_media_id` — matched by `media.id` and `ad_id` as built, not by the organic post; dynamic ads send no ad id; (i) a referral message whose ad automation is **held by its once key** is not handed on as a message (AC-20.6); (j) `message_edit` text is never re-matched; (k) the app dashboard must select exactly the six fields with "Include values" on (a `changed_fields` delivery is logged and saves nothing).

23. Found at the Close (2026-11-25): (a) a button tap that arrives before the hello's send result is dropped, so the run then waits for a tap she already gave (lane E's out-of-order test; WH11 assumes the tap comes after the message is saved); (b) DMs that expire behind a `plan_downgrade` stop end `expired` · `expired` (WC-15) — give them their own reason?; (c) the webhook store now opens up to 8 database connections per delivery ([P253](plans/decisions.md)) — confirm the API pool's headroom for concurrent deliveries; (d) the `hono` / `@hono/node-server` overrides added for `deps:audit:prod` may need the same change in meepzo-fe; (e) hosted environments and every developer's local env file need the switch's new name `LAPSED_PLAN_READ_ONLY_ENABLED` ([P148](plans/decisions.md)); `pnpm github:sync` pushes it; (f) a reply to a reply and a role change while a broadcast runs stay untested until decided (item 16 and the corner-case review).

## Standing deviations

The complete list of where the code differs from the design. This iteration has no Baseline but changes the launch in
place ([P29](plans/decisions.md)), so the list is the launch's (`../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/AS-BUILT.md` › Standing
deviations) minus what this iteration resolved (KICKOFF.md section 8: the token expiry, Instagram Login, the connect
refusals and settings, accounts per organization, the Member role, the daily account check, the email and phone checks,
automation delete, the launch automation form, next-post linking and expiry, the once rule, DM triggers, the 24-hour
rule, the opening channel, reply safety, the webhook-events keep time, the queues, the legacy settings, the launch's
account and token columns), with the launch rows it changed rewritten ("changed by"), plus its new ones (first). A
launch row's bare `D<n>` is the launch's decision; `P<n>` is this iteration's.

| Item | Design says | Built as | Since iteration | Decision |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| "Seen" after a reply | After a sent fast message, `mark_seen` when the account has it on (plan §30.13, WC-7) | Not sent: `mark_seen` stays off until its Meta test (P36) | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P412](plans/decisions.md) |
| Intake's commented-post read | Two optional Meta reads in intake (WH25), the post read moved to "next post" linking (P312 carry-over) | A third fail-open read kept: a commented post not stored yet is read and stored by intake (its publish time for `between`, its row for the missed-comment watch); the 15-minute linking sweep is added beside it | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P315](plans/decisions.md) |
| Malware scan of message files | Scanned before use (plan D58) | Type and content checks only | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P34](plans/decisions.md) |
| Chip tone `lav` gone | Renamed `lavender` everywhere (P81) | Stored and validated as `lavender`; an expand-phase trigger still turns a written `lav` into `lavender` until the release after this one drops it (contract) | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P202](plans/decisions.md) |
| `PASSWORD` auth-method type | Passwords removed end to end (P84) | Kept as a stored `method_type` for historic revoked rows (the CHECK accepts it); never created, accepted or returned — the API and OpenAPI use `LIVE_AUTH_METHOD_TYPES`; `auth.users.password_hash` stays, empty | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P209](plans/decisions.md) |
| Password routes | Removed with their code (P84) | Kept disabled behind `PASSWORD_ROUTES_ENABLED` (default `false` → not registered, 404); code marked `PASSWORD-ROUTES-DISABLED`; on, a reset sets `password_hash` again | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P251](plans/decisions.md) |
| Where the send work runs | The send workers as their own service, `worker-send` (plan, P91) | Every Instagram queue, the send family included, runs in the existing deployed `worker` service; the database limiter and the pool budget hold it | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P140](plans/decisions.md), [P143](plans/decisions.md) |
| Instagram account type and private flag | Shown on the account (plan §2) | `account_type` `null`, `is_private` `false`: Meta's Unified-Login profile read carries neither | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P120](plans/decisions.md) |
| The day limit | A per-account day limit for opening DMs | Stored and validated, but off while `DAY_LIMIT_ENABLED` is off (`is_day_limit_enabled` in the settings answer) | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P122](plans/decisions.md) |
| Message-access banner | "Turn on 'Allow access to messages'" when Meta answers message access off (plan §25.14) | Not built: no field holds it; a send that meets it stops the account's lanes, the set-up's probe only logs it | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P409](plans/decisions.md) |
| "Your DMs are safe" banner | Shown on the sending status while Redis or Postgres has been down over a minute (AC-27.6) | Not built: no route-63 field or detection rule | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P452](plans/decisions.md) |
| Restore step 4 | After a restore, the lost time's messages become `unknown` (DMs) or `skipped` (replies, hides) (plan §35) | Not built: a restored database sends nothing until switched on and a copy with another environment id never sends, but no step marks the lost time's rows | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P452](plans/decisions.md) |
| A picture with text | Counts as two DMs (AC-17.17) | The run engine saves one message row per list message, so it counts one | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P412](plans/decisions.md) |
| "Account no longer shared" on a send | A send answer that says so marks that account alone | Found by the daily token check (`account_not_shared`); no send answer maps to it until Meta's code is tested | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P252](plans/decisions.md), [P36](plans/decisions.md) |
| "The 500-waiting cap" | A cap of 500 waiting broadcast messages (plan) | Read as the audience built 500 people a piece; no other cap of 500 | feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [P451](plans/decisions.md) |
| API paths and folder names | `/app/…`; folders `social/instagram`, `automations`, `contacts` (schema-routes.md:45, :72) | `/api/v1/app/…`: the common `/app/social/channel-connections…` and `/app/social/channel-accounts…`, the shared product at the top (`/app/contacts…`, `/app/automations…`, `/app/broadcasts…`, `/app/segments…`, `/app/usage`), Instagram's own parts under `/app/social/instagram/…`; folders `channel-connection`, `channel-account`, `instagram-account`, `instagram-webhook`, `instagram-sending`, `automation`, `contact`, `broadcast` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D1](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D20](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P47](plans/decisions.md), [P48](plans/decisions.md) |
| Permission codes | `instagram_account:read` / `:manage`; `webhook:read` opens the webhook events (schema-routes.md:132-139) | `instagram-account:read` / `:manage` on the Instagram routes only; `channel-account:read` / `:manage` on the shared connection and account routes; `contact:update`; webhook events keep `instagram-webhook-event:read` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D4](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D20](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P20](plans/decisions.md), [P59](plans/decisions.md) |
| Personal organization | `422` on every route (schema-routes.md:74) | Writes `422`, reads `404 team_organization_required`; its Owner holds no code, so `403 missing_permission` comes first | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D25](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Read-only plan | No rule | Go live, a broadcast send and turning a PRO part on answer `403 organization_read_only`; edit, pause, delete and disconnect stay open | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D27](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P10](plans/decisions.md) |
| List search, sort and totals | `search`, repeated `tag`, `sort=-field`, `estimated_total` on lists (schema-routes.md:98, :329, :373-381) | `q`, comma-separated `tags` (10), `sort` + `order`; total on page one, on webhook events only with `include_total` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D25](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Saving over a newer version | No check (schema-routes.md:624) | Settings, automation, contact, segment and broadcast edits take `expected_updated_at`; an older one is `409 record_changed` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Audit entries | `instagram_account.connected`, `automation.created` …; connect's inside its transaction (schema-routes.md:448, :458, :614) | `app.instagram_account.connect`, `app.automation.create` …; always written after commit | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D20](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D36](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Account and settings objects | `username`, `profile_url` always set; status may be `revoked` (schema-routes.md:198-227) | Both may be null, never `revoked`; adds `is_webhook_subscribed`, `updated_at`; settings add `updated_by_user_id` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Read the account, change its settings | No route (schema-routes.md:26-39) | Per account: `GET /app/social/channel-accounts/{channel_account_id}` (`channel-account:read`) and `PATCH /app/social/instagram/accounts/{channel_account_id}/settings` (`instagram-account:manage`) | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D5](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P1](plans/decisions.md), [P47](plans/decisions.md) |
| Disconnect | No route; nothing sets `revoked` (schema-routes.md:221) | `DELETE …/channel-accounts/{channel_account_id}` and the business disconnect `DELETE …/channel-connections/{channel_connection_id}`: revoked, the account's lanes stopped, the token erased with the connection's last account; automations, contacts and history stay | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D5](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P1](plans/decisions.md), [P47](plans/decisions.md) |
| Deleting the team | Only the rows' cascade (schema-routes.md:684) | Over every account of the organization: unsubscribed, token, profile and stored posts erased, revoked, and their sending lanes stopped (R6 × R61 fix) | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D54](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Meta's deauthorize and data-deletion callbacks | None (schema-routes.md:26-39) | With the connection (`…/channel-connections/{provider}/deauthorize`, `…/data-deletion`), matched by the Instagram account id first, else every account of the business; the reached accounts' lanes stopped; an unmatched id still gets its code and the team an email | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D22](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D53](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P46](plans/decisions.md), [P56](plans/decisions.md) |
| Reconnect notice | None; only the status changes (schema-routes.md:488, :571) | In-app and email once per account and break; a broken connection tells every account of it; recipients Owner and Admin | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D10](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P41](plans/decisions.md), [P216](plans/decisions.md) |
| Instagram auth errors | Code 190 (463/467); on a send always `error` (schema-routes.md:488, :571) | One error-group table (Meta's codes → groups); a token error stops every account of the connection and breaks it once | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D32](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Post and story pickers | `409 instagram_reconnect_required`; `media_type`, `permalink`, `published_at` always set (schema-routes.md:240-263, :488) | `404` with that reason, no call once broken; those fields may be null; other Instagram refusals are `503` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D30](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Token isolation | Not readable by the normal app session (schema-routes.md:766) | As at launch, on the connection's token (`app.channel_connection_tokens`): the app role may select the encrypted row; only the client decrypts | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D21](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P49](plans/decisions.md) |
| Contacts per Instagram account | One per workspace and Instagram user (schema-routes.md:910) | One per channel account and Instagram user; `channel_account_id` (was `instagram_account_id`), required on the list | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D25](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P48](plans/decisions.md), [P65](plans/decisions.md) |
| Delete one contact | No route (schema-routes.md:646) | `DELETE …/contacts/{contact_id}` (`contact:delete`): data wiped, row kept, open runs, waiting messages, tag events and broadcast recipients cancelled or dropped; webhook events stay until their 8-day part drops | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D22](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D23](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D25](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Contact name, picture and followers | Columns without a source; events carry a username only (schema-routes.md:414, :897-899) | Read from Instagram once, for a new contact; a follow check saves only the follow answer | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D25](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Contact tags | The automation's tags or name added; at most 50 (schema-routes.md:557, :911) | A comma becomes a space; past 50, new tags are dropped and logged and the run completes | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D25](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Automations per Instagram account | Per workspace (schema-routes.md:834, :868) | Tied to the channel account named at create (`409 instagram_not_connected` when it is not connected); the way to start is required at create | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P48](plans/decisions.md), [P70](plans/decisions.md) |
| Next-post conflicts | Overlap across the workspace; linking fixes the window (schema-routes.md:612, :630) | Overlap per account, checked only when saved live; linking also fixes `target_scope` and the target ids | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Editing while runs are open | Not said | Open runs follow the edit at their next step; a step turned off is passed at the contact's next event inside the messaging window | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D48](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D50](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Automation `updated_at` | Set on every change (schema-routes.md:687) | The run counters do not move it, so an open edit does not go stale | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D30](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| How many automations answer a comment | Every matching live one (schema-routes.md:536) | One, by the match order of the 15 ways to start: post-linked first, else "any post", the oldest; every other match logged `ignored` · `comment_already_answered` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Email answers | Quick reply or first attachment; a wrong one gets the question again (schema-routes.md:537, :555) | Quick reply, any attachment or typed text; a wrong one gets one of up to 10 wordings (never the last one sent), asked again up to 3 times | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D18](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P19](plans/decisions.md) |
| Queued automations | All saved (schema-routes.md:555); started at the final DM (PRD.md:136) | One entry per automation, at most 100; started when the email arrives or its step is turned off | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D48](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Click on a finished run | Only `completed` ignores it (schema-routes.md:539); the others continue (PRD.md:112-113) | `completed`, `failed`, `cancelled` and `running` runs ignore it | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D18](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D49](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Wait limit and run retention | None; runs stay (schema-routes.md:640, :931) | A wait is cancelled after 7 days (`wait_expired`); runs, run events and comment logs split by month and dropped by part 90 days after they end | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D8](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P96](plans/decisions.md) |
| Same reply text twice | Never the automation's last reply text (schema-routes.md:551) | With a single reply text, that text is sent again | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | none (acceptance-gap row) |
| Token error on the public reply | Its failure never stops the flow (PRD.md:130) | Stops every account of the connection and sends the reconnect notice; the message waits in its stopped lane | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Webhook proof failures | `403` for a wrong verify token or signature (schema-routes.md:512, :525, :669) | `400 invalid_webhook_verify_token` / `invalid_webhook_signature`, in the API's error shape | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Webhook rate limit | None (schema-routes.md:84) | Per IP for unsigned traffic only (a signed request is not counted); POST body at most 5 MB | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D21](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P97](plans/decisions.md) |
| Store before answering | `200 OK` at once, even when something fails (PRD.md:20-22, schema-routes.md:530) | Events are stored first (no Redis buffer); a store failure or a slow store answers `500` / `503`, so Meta delivers again | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D15](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P32](plans/decisions.md) |
| Event of an unknown or revoked account | Stored without an organization as `unknown_account`, never listed (schema-routes.md:528, :814) | Not stored: a log line and a metric; still `200 OK` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Event keys | Only for comments, messages, postbacks; unique across accounts (schema-routes.md:817, :825) | Every kind has a key, in its own table split by Meta's event day, kept 8 days | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D15](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P96](plans/decisions.md) |
| Skip reasons | `unknown_account` listed; `unsupported` has no rule (schema-routes.md:528, :1012) | No `unknown_account`; other fields and deleted messages are `unsupported`; the account's own comments are `own_comment`; more than 20 of her message events a minute `flood` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D18](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D19](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P24](plans/decisions.md), [P63](plans/decisions.md) |
| Stored webhook text | The event kept as received (schema-routes.md:820) | NUL characters and lone surrogates are cleaned out first; one bad entry never stops a delivery's other entries | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D53](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Webhook-events list | Payload as received; kind and status plain strings (schema-routes.md:273-283) | Payload masked, and `null` once the event is done; adds `instagram_user_id`, `contact_instagram_user_id`, `channel_account_id`, `attempt_count`, `occurred_at`, `next_attempt_at`; kind and status typed lists; kept 8 days | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D19](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| `instagram_media` columns | `owner_username`, `shortcode`, `is_comment_enabled`; per organization (schema-routes.md:789-804) | `instagram_account_id` added, `owner_username` dropped; Instagram is not asked for `shortcode` or `is_comment_enabled` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D30](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| `instagram_webhook_events` columns | `organization_id` nullable; a `received_at` column (schema-routes.md:814-822) | Organization required; `received_at` is `created_at`; adds `instagram_account_id`, `contact_instagram_user_id`, `occurred_at`, `attempt_count`, `last_attempted_at`, `request_id` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D28](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Run, contact and automation columns | The drop's columns (schema-routes.md:857–949) | Shared tables keyed by `channel_account_id`; the send claim moved from the run to the message row (`app.instagram_messages`); automations reshaped (status `draft` / `live` / `paused`, `steps`, per-version counters) | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D25](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P48](plans/decisions.md) |
| Comment-log references and writing | Run and contact unreferenced; saved outside the run's transaction (schema-routes.md:953-963) | Both referenced, contact required; written in the run's own short units; three unread indexes dropped | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D21](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Comment-log value lists | `waiting_for` has `button`; no `cancelled` (schema-routes.md:987, :1024-1027) | `opening_message_button`; adds `overall_status` `cancelled` and `ignored_reason` `comment_already_answered` | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D19](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Comment-log error columns | `error_response` and `error_stack` saved (schema-routes.md:993-994) | `error_stack` is never written; `error_response` keeps Instagram's class, codes and trace id only, never its text | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D53](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Row-level security | A session sees and writes only its workspace's rows (schema-routes.md:686) | Plus a maintenance clean-up arm (not on tokens) and two resolvers returning only organization ids | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D21](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D24](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Index and key names | `idx_<table>_<cols>`, `_unique` endings, `<table>_<col>_fk` (schema-routes.md:693, :741) | No `_unique`, composite keys `<table>_<parent>_fk`, the organization first in child unique keys, names cut to 63 bytes | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D30](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Skipped entries and duplicates | An echo skips its whole entry; duplicates only logged; same-millisecond entry dropped (PRD.md:30-36) | Only that event is skipped; each event is stored once by its key, none dropped | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D15](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Retries | 3 tries, 2 s then 4 s; handler errors only logged (PRD.md:42-44, :84) | 5 tries from 5 s, then the event is `failed`; a failed event is retried every hour until its comment is 7 days old | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D16](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D47](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D51](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Sending again on a retry | Messages may go twice, logged DUPLICATE RISK (PRD.md:66) | Each send is claimed first; an unclear answer is saved `unknown` and never sent again by any job — her next message or tap may send that step once more, as the same row | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D16](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D55](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Personal data in logs | Message text previewed, emails and phones masked (PRD.md:176) | Message text, names, usernames, emails, phones and the contact note are never logged | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D21](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D38](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D40](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Reading runs and comment logs | Public ids `run` and `acl` for both (schema-routes.md:164-165) | No run or comment-log route; a contact's "What happened" (#24) shows her messages and runs; the webhook-events list stays the debugging view | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D13](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Data from the legacy system | A running legacy system with its own data (PRD.md:5) | Not imported; organizations reconnect Instagram and create their automations again | launch-2026-11-20-ujai | [D7](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md) |
| Live updates, plan limits, export, restore | Not said | Plan limits added (accounts, the month's DMs, PRO switches); live updates, contact export and restore of a deleted automation or contact still not built | launch-2026-11-20-ujai · changed by feat-instagram-dm-sending-2026-11-23-oxak | [D11](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D12](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D25](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [D26](../launch-2026-11-20-ujai/plans/decisions.md), [P83](plans/decisions.md) |
